third_party/craft-fonts/ATTRIBUTION.md

# Font attribution

Every font in `fonts/`, with its source and licence. The files are byte-identical to the pinned
upstream sources (checked by `craft-fonts-tests`, which verifies each SHA-256 in the manifest that
lists it: `fonts/manifest.txt` for the default embedded set, `fonts/latin-manifest.txt` for the
opt-in Latin presentation faces, and `fonts/simplified-chinese-manifest.txt` for the opt-in
native-only Simplified Chinese face).

The OFL allows these fonts to be bundled, embedded and redistributed with software, provided the
licence text travels with them and the fonts are not sold on their own. "Shippori Mincho",
"BIZ UD", "Noto" and "LXGW WenKai Lite" are the authors' names for their fonts; we use the files
unmodified. LXGW WenKai Lite's upstream OFL reserves the names 霞鹜, 霞鶩, 落霞孤鹜, 落霞孤鶩 and
LXGW; the bundled release member and Klee-One copyright notice stay byte-identical to upstream.

The Symbols variable font keeps its upstream bytes; only its filename changes from
`NotoSansSymbols[wght].ttf` to `NotoSansSymbols.ttf`.

## Latin presentation fonts (`fonts/latin-manifest.txt`)

Opt-in fonts for UI, presentation and document apps (see `docs/integration.md`): 16 families and 51 faces, about 19.5 MiB (20,417,364 bytes of font files). All are OFL-1.1.
Variable-font files are renamed without their `[axes]` suffix (e.g. `Montserrat[wght].ttf` is
`Montserrat.ttf`); each licence file is the upstream licence text (upstream `LICENSE.txt`,
`LICENSE` or `LICENSE.md` for Inter, Liberation and Source) saved as `OFL.txt`. Font files are
byte-identical to upstream.

"Inter", "Carlito", "Caladea", "Liberation", "Source", "Montserrat", "Lato", "Open Sans", "Roboto",
"Merriweather", "Playfair Display", "Poppins" and "Nunito Sans" are the authors' names for their
fonts (several are Reserved Font Names under the OFL); we use the files unmodified. Carlito,
Caladea and Liberation are metric-compatible with Calibri, Cambria, Arial, Times New Roman and
Courier New; those names are trademarks of their owners and are used only to describe
compatibility.

The eight **Adobe-authored** Source Sans 3 and Source Serif 4 faces remain in this central catalog
for provenance and reuse, but consumers that must not embed Adobe fonts filter those two families
(8 of the 51 faces, 2,594,420 bytes) and keep the remaining 43. DeckCraft already applies that
filter; FigCraft adds it in its own build. Filtering is a consumer obligation and does not change
the manifest or these pinned hashes.

third_party/craft-fonts/README.md

craft-fonts
===========

<a href="https://sonarcloud.io/project/overview?id=storytold_craft-fonts"><img alt="Scanned by SonarCloud" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Scanned_by-SonarCloud-4b9fd5?style=flat-square&logo=sonarqubecloud&logoColor=white"></a>

Reusable fonts for the Crafting Apps.

This library is meant to have the following:

1. Reusable font assets. These may be large files, and since we're using a separate repo for storing them,
   We're not afraid of potentially having to do a git reset to purge stale assets. It won't impact the other
   repos with core app and library functionality.

2. Additional cross-cutting Rust libraries for dealing with fonts. It's uncertain whether it's better to
   have code packages as individual repos as one classically does, or whether we should take a
   more "biology-inspired" approach of copying innovations between repos as a form of lateral code/information
   exchange.

## What's here

| Path | What |
|---|---|
| `fonts/` | The font files, one directory per family, each with its licence. |
| `fonts/manifest.txt` | Machine-readable list of the default fonts: family, style, file, scripts, licence, SHA-256, pinned source. |
| `fonts/latin-manifest.txt` | Opt-in Latin presentation catalog, same eight-field format: 16 families and 51 faces (about 19.5 MiB). Not in the default `FONTS` or `embed` payload. |
| `fonts/simplified-chinese-manifest.txt` | Opt-in native-only Simplified Chinese row, same eight-field format. Not in `FONTS` or the embed payload. |
| `ATTRIBUTION.md` | Authors, pinned sources and licences. |
| `crates/craft-fonts` | Rust: the default and opt-in Latin manifests as static tables, finding a checkout (`CRAFT_FONTS_DIR`), loading them, and `embed` / `embed-latin` features that compile the fonts in. |
| `crates/craft-fonts-tests` | Tests asserted on these fonts: checksums, licences, family/style names, Inter/Latin provenance and faces, Japanese/Korean/Hebrew/Devanagari/symbol coverage and outlines, static-face metadata and vertical-text (`vert`/`vrt2`) support. |
| `docs/integration.md` | How an app uses craft-fonts as a fully optional build input. |

### Fonts

| Family | Styles | For | Licence |
|---|---|---|---|
| BIZ UDPGothic | Regular, Bold | Japanese UI text (sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| Shippori Mincho | Regular | Japanese document text (serif) | OFL-1.1 |
| BIZ UDMincho | Regular | Japanese document text (serif) | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans CJK SC | Regular | Simplified Chinese UI and document text (sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans Arabic | Regular (variable: wdth, wght) | Arabic UI and document text (sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans Thai | Regular (variable: wdth, wght) | Thai UI and document text (sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans Georgian | Regular (variable: wdth, wght) | Georgian UI and document text (sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| NanumGothic | Regular | Korean UI and document text | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans Symbols | Regular (variable: wght) | UI key/modifier symbols (`⌃`, arrows) | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans Symbols 2 | Regular | UI symbols (`✕`, `◀`, `✓`) | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans Hebrew | Regular (static) | Hebrew UI and document text | OFL-1.1 |
| Noto Sans Devanagari | Regular (static) | Devanagari UI and document text | OFL-1.1 |
| LXGW WenKai Lite | Regular (static) | Native-only opt-in Simplified Chinese fallback (Kai) | OFL-1.1 |

### Latin presentation fonts (`fonts/latin-manifest.txt`, opt-in)

These are not part of the default `FONTS` table or `embed` payload. Apps opt in by reading the
second manifest in their own `build.rs` (see [`docs/integration.md`](docs/integration.md)).
The central catalog is 16 families, 51 faces, about 19.5 MiB (20,417,364 bytes of font files).
"Variable" means one variable-font file (weight axis, some also width or optical size) per
upright/italic.

| Family | Styles | For | Licence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Inter | Regular, Italic, SemiBold, Bold | UI and document text (sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| Carlito | Regular, Bold, Italic, Bold Italic | Metric-compatible with Calibri | OFL-1.1 |
| Caladea | Regular, Bold, Italic, Bold Italic | Metric-compatible with Cambria | OFL-1.1 |
| Liberation Sans | Regular, Bold, Italic, Bold Italic | Metric-compatible with Arial | OFL-1.1 |
| Liberation Serif | Regular, Bold, Italic, Bold Italic | Metric-compatible with Times New Roman | OFL-1.1 |
| Liberation Mono | Regular, Bold, Italic, Bold Italic | Metric-compatible with Courier New | OFL-1.1 |
| Source Sans 3 | Regular, Italic, Semibold, Bold | Presentation text (sans; Adobe-authored, catalog-only for these consumers) | OFL-1.1 |
| Source Serif 4 | Regular, Italic, Semibold, Bold | Presentation text (serif; Adobe-authored, catalog-only for these consumers) | OFL-1.1 |
| Montserrat | Variable, Italic Variable | Headings (geometric sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| Lato | Regular, Bold, Italic | Presentation text (sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| Open Sans | Variable, Italic Variable | Presentation text (sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| Roboto | Variable, Italic Variable | Presentation text (sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| Merriweather | Regular, Bold, Italic | Presentation text (serif) | OFL-1.1 |
| Playfair Display | Variable, Italic Variable | Headings (display serif) | OFL-1.1 |
| Poppins | Regular, Bold, Italic | Headings (geometric sans) | OFL-1.1 |
| Nunito Sans | Variable, Italic Variable | Presentation text (sans) | OFL-1.1 |

The eight Adobe-authored Source Sans 3 / Source Serif 4 faces stay in this central catalog for
provenance and reuse. Native consumers that must not embed Adobe fonts filter those two families,
leaving the 43 permitted Latin faces; DeckCraft already filters them and FigCraft adds the same
family filter in its own build. The catalog and its hashes do not depend on any consumer filter.

## How the apps use it

**Fully optional, never required.** The apps don't list craft-fonts in `Cargo.toml` (Cargo would
fetch it for every build). Building with `CRAFT_FONTS_DIR=<path to this checkout>` makes an app
embed the fonts in `fonts/manifest.txt`; without it, the app builds and runs as before. Release
builds check out a pinned commit of this repo and set the option. Apps that explicitly opt in may
also read `fonts/latin-manifest.txt` (the full Latin catalog, filtered by the consumer) and, on
native builds only, `fonts/simplified-chinese-manifest.txt`. Neither is in the default set; only
the Chinese manifest is native-only and must never enter a wasm allowlist. A consumer that may
not embed Adobe-authored faces filters `Source Sans 3` and `Source Serif 4` from the Latin rows.
See [`docs/integration.md`](docs/integration.md).

**Font assets for the Crafting Apps belong here**, not in the app repos: add the file, its licence,
a manifest line and an attribution row, and the tests check it. Put a Latin presentation face in
`fonts/latin-manifest.txt` and a face that only some native consumers should embed in
`fonts/simplified-chinese-manifest.txt`; neither belongs in the default `fonts/manifest.txt`.

## Develop

```sh
cargo test --workspace                       # all font assertions
cargo test -p craft-fonts --features embed   # the embedded bytes match the files
cargo test -p craft-fonts --features embed-latin  # same, for the opt-in Latin manifest
```

Code is MIT OR Apache-2.0 (`LICENSE-MIT`, `LICENSE-APACHE`); each font keeps its own licence.

third_party/craft-i18n/ATTRIBUTION.md

# Translation attribution

Everyone who has translated strings in `locales/`, by locale, and the app commit each migrated
catalog came from (`<app>@<sha>`). Translations are contributed under the repository's licence
(MIT OR Apache-2.0). English is the source text in the apps and is not listed.

## WordCraft egui 0.37 migration additions

@skylartaylor contributed the `Delete Around Caret in Equation` row in `bg`, `cs`, `da`,
`de`, `es`, `et`, `fr`, `hr`, `hu`, `it`, `ja`, `ka`, `ko`, `nl`, `pl`, `pt-br`, `ro`,
`ru`, `sl`, `sr`, `sr-latn`, `sv`, `tr`, `uk`, `vi`, `zh-hans` and `zh-hant`. The original 26 rows were prepared in the unpublished WordCraft migration and moved
here after the app adopted shared catalogs. The same command label was then added
to the Georgian catalog. The revised wording names the caret within the
equation, where the command deletes adjacent editing units. They were written from
the English meaning; no native-speaker review is recorded. They are separate from the earlier catalog
migrations and translator credits above.

# Attribution

Every asset DeckCraft includes, bundles, embeds at build time or shows in its documentation, with
its author, source and licence. The policy is in [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md) §1. `cargo xtask assets`
fails if a file under `assets/`, `docs/images/` or `docs/brand/` is missing from this table.

DeckCraft contains **no Microsoft, Adobe, Avid or Autodesk iconography, images, fonts, themes,
templates or other assets**. Its themes, colour schemes, layouts, preset-shape geometry, sample
deck and UI icons are original work of the DeckCraft contributors (drawn or generated in code).
Icons placed from Insert › Icons are Lucide paths (ISC; Feather-derived icons MIT), not Microsoft
artwork. The ribbon and menu icons stay original drawings.

| `crates/render/tests/fixtures/colr-squares.ttf` | DeckCraft contributors | two squares as a COLRv0/CPAL test font, built by `make_colr_font.py` (fontTools as the builder only) | CC0-1.0 | Colour glyph rendering tests |
| `crates/fonts/tests/fixtures/legacy-semibold.ttf` | DeckCraft contributors | a one-glyph Semibold face named like Segoe UI Semibold (its Windows family differs from the typographic one), built by `make_legacy_font.py` (fontTools as the builder only) | CC0-1.0 | Font lookup by Windows family name tests |
| `crates/fonts/tests/fixtures/legacy-regular.ttf` | DeckCraft contributors | the same family's one-glyph Regular face, built by `make_legacy_font.py` (fontTools as the builder only) | CC0-1.0 | Font lookup by Windows family name tests |
| `crates/render/tests/fixtures/make_colr_font.py` | DeckCraft contributors | this repository | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | Regenerates the COLR test font |
| Lucide icon paths in `crates/engine/src/icon_data.rs` (Insert › Icons) | Lucide Contributors; Feather icons by Cole Bemis | https://github.com/lucide-icons/lucide/tree/440a1e1dfb1e712505308da46bead3c28b1f1398/icons (release 1.56.0, 2026-10-13) | ISC; MIT for the Feather-derived icons named in that commit's LICENSE | Stroke icons inserted as shapes |

## Bundled through dependencies

| Asset | Author | Source | Licence | Used for |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Ubuntu Light (`Ubuntu-Light.ttf`, via the `epaint_default_fonts` crate 0.37) | Dalton Maag Ltd for Canonical | https://crates.io/crates/epaint_default_fonts | Ubuntu Font Licence 1.0 | Last-resort text face when no other font is available; UI fallback |
| Hack Regular, Noto Emoji (via `epaint_default_fonts` / egui) | Source Foundry; Google | https://crates.io/crates/epaint_default_fonts | MIT / Bitstream-Vera, OFL-1.1 | egui UI fallback glyphs |
| `epaint_default_fonts::EGUI_ICONS`, `epaint_default_fonts::EMOJI_ICON` (dependency font inputs) | John Slegers | [egui 6d518fc7](https://github.com/storytold/egui/tree/6d518fc799681571963842f95b55a6b68431b15a/crates/epaint_default_fonts/fonts), [emoji-icon-font](https://github.com/jslegers/emoji-icon-font) | MIT (Copyright (c) 2014 John Slegers; full upstream text in `NOTICE`) | egui-icons.ttf is the five-glyph subset, included by default_fonts independently of monochrome_emoji_fonts; emoji-icon-font.ttf is the full monochrome fallback. Dependency assets unmodified here; no font file copied into this repository. |

## Embedded at build time from craft-fonts (`CRAFT_FONTS_DIR`)

Release builds (and local builds with a craft-fonts checkout) embed the fonts listed in
[craft-fonts](https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts) `fonts/latin-manifest.txt` and
`fonts/manifest.txt` (per-file authors, sources, SHA-256 and licences in its
[ATTRIBUTION.md](https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts/blob/main/ATTRIBUTION.md)), except the
families excluded by `crates/fonts/build.rs`:

| Families | Licence | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Inter | OFL-1.1 | Default theme font |
| Carlito, Caladea | OFL-1.1 | Metric-compatible stand-ins for documents that ask for Calibri / Cambria |
| Liberation Sans, Serif, Mono | OFL-1.1 | Metric-compatible stand-ins for Arial, Times New Roman, Courier New |
| Montserrat, Lato, Open Sans, Roboto, Merriweather, Playfair Display, Poppins, Nunito Sans | OFL-1.1 | Theme fonts |
| BIZ UDPGothic, BIZ UDMincho, Shippori Mincho | OFL-1.1 | Japanese text |
| Noto Sans Arabic, Noto Sans Hebrew | OFL-1.1 | Arabic and Hebrew text |
| Noto Sans Thai, Noto Sans Georgian | OFL-1.1 | Thai and Georgian text |
| NanumGothic | OFL-1.1 | Korean text and interface fallback |
| Noto Sans Symbols, Noto Sans Symbols 2 | OFL-1.1 | Interface symbol fallback |

Web (wasm32) builds embed only Inter Regular/Bold (and a Variable face where the Latin manifest
provides one) and BIZ UDPGothic Regular.

## PDF writer colour profiles

`craft-pdf` is used with only its `write` feature and default features disabled. Krilla
0.8.2 embeds the following profiles; these were already reachable through the direct Krilla
exporter. No interpreter fallback fonts or CMaps are enabled, and no profile or font binary
is copied into this repository. The licence text is in `licenses/pdf-writer/LICENSE-ICC.txt`.

| Asset | Author | Source | Licence | SHA-256 | Used for |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `krilla 0.8.2 / icc/sGrey-v2-magic.icc` | Clinton Ingram (Compact ICC Profiles) | https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla/blob/3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5/crates/krilla/icc/sGrey-v2-magic.icc | CC0-1.0 | `4110169287e5f1b5149ca9f1da1814d32943905bba8030e849d1a3b729a19481` | PDF export colour profiles |
| `krilla 0.8.2 / icc/sGrey-v4.icc` | Clinton Ingram (Compact ICC Profiles) | https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla/blob/3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5/crates/krilla/icc/sGrey-v4.icc | CC0-1.0 | `00c0f94e09127520a17dc0e1d9264b5702081d96dbdb1549ee88e3631ce42a9d` | PDF export colour profiles |
| `krilla 0.8.2 / icc/sRGB-v2-magic.icc` | Clinton Ingram (Compact ICC Profiles) | https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla/blob/3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5/crates/krilla/icc/sRGB-v2-magic.icc | CC0-1.0 | `af4efe28f6d311799865f325ba39184a2e978b113ca74124d60af7de22b105f4` | PDF export colour profiles |
| `krilla 0.8.2 / icc/sRGB-v4.icc` | Clinton Ingram (Compact ICC Profiles) | https://github.com/LaurenzV/krilla/blob/3ffdf0588cf98050aad6edba51ca70162e1fb5b5/crates/krilla/icc/sRGB-v4.icc | CC0-1.0 | `c56e1685d888f5edb92fe07f2750f387f8fe8e91b32ff8fb0b56bfbbb9458353` | PDF export colour profiles |

### Optional native Chinese fallback

Native builds also read craft-fonts' `fonts/simplified-chinese-manifest.txt` when present.
This opt-in does not enter the wasm font allowlist. Font bytes and the full licence remain
in craft-fonts, including the LXGW Reserved Font Names and original Klee notice.

| Family | Author | Source | Licence | Use |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| LXGW WenKai Lite Regular 1.530 | LXGW; original Klee Project Authors / Fontworks | https://github.com/lxgw/LxgwWenKai-Lite/tree/b712c83d98acf977bacfb7fb010207802808bda9 | OFL-1.1, unmodified upstream font | Native Chinese glyph fallback after existing UI faces; font SHA256 `f799a538a3355877e597a0ae04050e897932c9ae7689eb9227c2953c5dd549ea`, OFL SHA256 `0333a85fe077b6849f8e417acc44e8bd879442ceb681e271acf93e2d57fd4e2e` |

# Asset attribution

Every non-code asset in this repository (images, icons, fonts, example files, presets) is listed here with its author, source and licence. `cargo xtask assets` (part of `cargo xtask ci`) fails if an asset file is missing from this table.

**Policy (mandatory):** GridCraft contains **no Microsoft, Adobe, Avid or Autodesk iconography, images, artwork, fonts, templates, themes, table styles or sample files**. Every asset is original work by GridCraft contributors or third-party material under an open licence (OSI open source, public domain / CC0, or Creative Commons that allows redistribution). Screenshots of Microsoft software are never committed. Font files are not added here: shared fonts live in [storytold/craft-fonts](https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts), an optional build input (`CRAFT_FONTS_DIR`). The one exception is the ArtCraft brand files in `docs/brand/`: ArtCraft trademarks, not open source, used under `docs/brand/LICENSE-brand.txt`.

Generated-in-code art is original and has no file to list: the UI icon set (`crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs`), themes (`crates/engine/src/cmd/view.rs`), cell styles (`crates/engine/src/cmd/format.rs`), table styles (`crates/engine/src/tables.rs`), chart palettes and the sample workbooks (`crates/engine/src/sample.rs`).

## Folio Android interface font

Inter Regular and SemiBold by Rasmus Andersson, OFL-1.1, are an optional build input from the pinned WordCraft source in Folio `engine-lock.json` (`ui_fonts`). No font files are committed to this fork. Folio verifies each SHA-256 before compilation and retains `OFL-Inter.txt` in its notices. Used only for the interface; document fonts are unchanged.

| Build input | Author | Pinned source | Licence | SHA-256 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `FOLIO_UI_FONT_DIR/Inter-Regular.ttf` | Rasmus Andersson | https://github.com/storytold/wordcraft/blob/9130181a5fdaee2293e67756fd5458c601489f3c/assets/fonts/Inter-Regular.ttf | OFL-1.1 (`OFL-Inter.txt`, supplied by Folio) | `40d692fce188e4471e2b3cba937be967878f631ad3ebbbdcd587687c7ebe0c82` |
| `FOLIO_UI_FONT_DIR/Inter-SemiBold.ttf` | Rasmus Andersson | https://github.com/storytold/wordcraft/blob/9130181a5fdaee2293e67756fd5458c601489f3c/assets/fonts/Inter-SemiBold.ttf | OFL-1.1 (`OFL-Inter.txt`, supplied by Folio) | `78a843fade9d4612a5567302fb595b56976eb5fcebf4fea5a5912d638bafcde3` |

ArtCraft brand assets: license

The ArtCraft name, wordmark and mark (the "ArtCraft Marks"), including every file in this
directory in every color, size and format, are owned by the ArtCraft Team. They are trademarks
and are NOT open source. They are not covered by GridCraft's MIT OR Apache-2.0 license, and nothing in
that license, in NOTICE or in any other file of this repository grants any right to them.

Permission

The ArtCraft Team grants a limited, non-exclusive, non-transferable, revocable, royalty-free
permission to use the ArtCraft Marks only:

  1. as part of copies of this repository; and
  2. within GridCraft itself, its documentation and its official builds, to identify GridCraft as made
     by the ArtCraft team and community and to link to getartcraft.com and its community.

Conditions

The permission holds only while every one of these is true:

  - The marks are used exactly as supplied in this directory. Do not redraw, recolor, crop,
    stretch, rotate, animate, outline or add effects to them, extract parts of them, or combine
    them with any other name, mark or artwork.
  - They are used only in the context of GridCraft, and only to say that the ArtCraft team makes it.
    They must never suggest that the ArtCraft Team made, sponsors, endorses, certifies or supports
    any other product, service, fork, modified version, plug-in, organization or person.
  - Neither the marks nor the ArtCraft name appear in the name, logo, icon, domain, social media
    handle, app-store listing, advertising or marketing of anything other than GridCraft.

Forks and modified versions

If you distribute or publish a modified version of GridCraft, or any work derived from it, you must
remove the ArtCraft Marks from it (or replace them with your own) and must not call it ArtCraft or
present it as an ArtCraft product. You may say, in plain text, that your work is based on GridCraft by
the ArtCraft team. A fork kept only to propose changes back to this repository may keep the marks
unchanged.

(https://getartcraft.com/). The permission ends automatically for anyone who breaks these terms.

| `crates/fonts/src/std14_metrics.rs` | Standard-14 font advance widths and encodings (as Rust tables) | Adobe (Core14 AFM metrics, PDF specification encodings); tables generated by the PdfCraft contributors | MIT OR Apache-2.0 | ISO 32000-2 Annex D and the Adobe Core14 AFM metrics. Generated by joining the published WinAnsi, Symbol and ZapfDingbats encodings (code to glyph name) with the per-glyph advance widths, then spot-checked against the specification by hand in the tests of crates/fonts/src/std14.rs. Data only: the code that reads these tables lives in std14.rs, so that ordinary edits do not churn this file's hash. | Advance widths for the fourteen standard fonts, which a PDF may name without embedding. # ATTRIBUTION.md is generated from this file: `cargo xtask assets --write`. Checked by `cargo xtask assets` (part of `xtask ci`).
#
# [[asset]]    a file in this repository (committed or vendored); `sha256` must match the file.
#              Translation catalogs (kind = "translation", crates/ui-egui/src/i18n/*.tsv) are project files
#              under the project licence and carry no `sha256`: git history records their provenance.
# [[bundled]]  a file compiled into PdfCraft by a Cargo dependency (its crate version must match Cargo.lock).
# [[fetched]]  downloaded at build time by xtask (never committed), pinned by `sha256`.
# [[build_input]] an optional build input outside this repository (e.g. craft-fonts via CRAFT_FONTS_DIR);
#              never committed or downloaded here, its own repository attributes each file.
#
# licence: SPDX expression from the allowlist in AGENTS.md §1.2. adobe_data = true marks the closed list of
# Adobe-authored non-visual technical data allowed by AGENTS.md §1.1.

Third-party material

This product bundles third-party material under its own licenses, listed below. The per-file
list (author, source, license) is in ATTRIBUTION.md.

- Lucide icons (assets/icons/*.svg) — ISC License. See assets/icons/LICENSE-lucide.txt.
  The Windows PDF file icon (assets/app-icon/pdfcraft-document.svg and .ico) adapts Lucide's
  file-text outline and combines it with contributor-original artwork. See
  assets/app-icon/LICENSE-document.txt (ISC AND (MIT OR Apache-2.0)), packaged as
  LICENSE-document.txt beside the Windows executables.
- Inter typeface (assets/fonts/Inter-*.ttf) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. See assets/fonts/OFL-Inter.txt.
- JetBrains Mono typeface (assets/fonts/JetBrainsMono-*.ttf) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. See assets/fonts/OFL-JetBrainsMono.txt.
- Dancing Script typeface (assets/fonts/DancingScript.ttf), Copyright 2016 The Dancing Script Project Authors — SIL Open Font License 1.1 (Reserved Font Name "Dancing Script"). See assets/fonts/OFL-DancingScript.txt.
- Fonts from craft-fonts (https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts), an optional build input
  (CRAFT_FONTS_DIR) that is not part of this repository. Builds made with it, which includes every
  official release, embed the fonts listed in
  https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts/blob/main/ATTRIBUTION.md (BIZ UDPGothic, Shippori Mincho,
  BIZ UDMincho; the web build only BIZ UDPGothic Regular) under the SIL Open Font License 1.1. Those
  packages carry each font's licence as OFL-<family>.txt. Builds made with the craft-fonts revision
  pinned in .github/workflows/ also include Noto Sans Thai (in the web build too), Copyright 2022
  The Noto Project Authors (https://github.com/notofonts/thai), under SIL OFL 1.1; its licence is
  fonts/noto-sans-thai/OFL.txt in the craft-fonts build input.
- hayro (rendering, via crates.io) — Apache-2.0 OR MIT. Its `embed-fonts` feature bundles the Foxit
  standard-14 Type 1 fonts under BSD-3-Clause ("Copyright 2014 PDFium Authors"), and hayro-cmap bundles
  Adobe CMap resources under BSD-3-Clause.
- lopdf (bootstrap document inspection, via crates.io) — MIT.
- egui default fonts (epaint_default_fonts, via eframe) — Ubuntu Light (Ubuntu Font Licence 1.0),
  Hack (MIT / Bitstream Vera licence), Noto Emoji (SIL OFL 1.1), emoji-icon-font (MIT).
- Colour profile in hayro-interpret — CGATS001Compat-v2-micro.icc (CC0-1.0, Clinton Ingram).

Required notices:

  Foxit standard-14 substitute fonts (vendor/hayro-interpret/assets/*.pfb):
    Copyright 2014 PDFium Authors. All rights reserved. Original code copyright 2014 Foxit Software Inc.
    Redistributed under the BSD-3-Clause licence in vendor/hayro-interpret/assets/LICENSE_FOXIT.

  CMap resources (bundled by hayro-cmap; character-mapping data, not visual assets):
    Copyright 1990-2023 Adobe. All rights reserved. Redistributed under the BSD-3-Clause licence
    (https://github.com/adobe-type-tools/cmap-resources/blob/master/LICENSE.txt). Neither the name of
    Adobe nor the names of its contributors are used to endorse or promote this product.

Showcase PDF (built by `cargo xtask demo-pdf`, not committed): fonts are downloaded at build time from
google/fonts at a pinned commit, verified by SHA-256, all under the SIL Open Font License 1.1 — see the
"Downloaded at build time" table in ATTRIBUTION.md.

Asset licences are not visible to cargo-deny; keep this file current whenever assets or asset-bundling
dependencies change (plan/adr/0002, 0006).

Vendored (vendor/, patched; see vendor/README.md):
- hayro 0.7.1 — Apache-2.0 OR MIT, Copyright the hayro authors (licence files kept in vendor/hayro).
- hayro-interpret 0.7.0 — Apache-2.0 OR MIT, Copyright the hayro authors (LICENSE-APACHE, LICENSE-MIT kept in vendor/hayro-interpret).

Bundled egui font icons

The pinned egui dependency includes egui-icons.ttf, a small subset of John Slegers'
emoji-icon-font, through default_fonts. The parent emoji-icon-font.ttf face is
included through monochrome_emoji_fonts. Their upstream project is
https://github.com/jslegers/emoji-icon-font; both use the same exact copyright
and permission notice below.

The icon uses no typeface or external image. Regenerate the ICO with
packaging/icons.sh; every asset and its SHA-256 is recorded in ATTRIBUTION.toml.

ArtCraft brand assets: license

The ArtCraft name, wordmark and mark (the "ArtCraft Marks"), including every file in this
directory in every color, size and format, are owned by the ArtCraft Team. They are trademarks
and are NOT open source. They are not covered by PdfCraft's MIT OR Apache-2.0 license, and nothing in
that license, in NOTICE or in any other file of this repository grants any right to them.

Permission

The ArtCraft Team grants a limited, non-exclusive, non-transferable, revocable, royalty-free
permission to use the ArtCraft Marks only:

  1. as part of copies of this repository; and
  2. within PdfCraft itself, its documentation and its official builds, to identify PdfCraft as made
     by the ArtCraft team and community and to link to getartcraft.com and its community.

Conditions

The permission holds only while every one of these is true:

  - The marks are used exactly as supplied in this directory. Do not redraw, recolor, crop,
    stretch, rotate, animate, outline or add effects to them, extract parts of them, or combine
    them with any other name, mark or artwork.
  - They are used only in the context of PdfCraft, and only to say that the ArtCraft team makes it.
    They must never suggest that the ArtCraft Team made, sponsors, endorses, certifies or supports
    any other product, service, fork, modified version, plug-in, organization or person.
  - Neither the marks nor the ArtCraft name appear in the name, logo, icon, domain, social media
    handle, app-store listing, advertising or marketing of anything other than PdfCraft.

Forks and modified versions

If you distribute or publish a modified version of PdfCraft, or any work derived from it, you must
remove the ArtCraft Marks from it (or replace them with your own) and must not call it ArtCraft or
present it as an ArtCraft product. You may say, in plain text, that your work is based on PdfCraft by
the ArtCraft team. A fork kept only to propose changes back to this repository may keep the marks
unchanged.

Everything else

Any use not expressly permitted here needs prior written permission from the ArtCraft Team
(https://getartcraft.com/). The permission ends automatically for anyone who breaks these terms.
All other rights are reserved.

third_party/wordcraft/ATTRIBUTION.md

# Asset attribution

Every non-code asset in this repository (fonts, icons, images, example documents) is listed here with its author, source and licence. `cargo xtask assets` (part of `cargo xtask ci`) fails if an asset file is missing from this table.

**Policy (mandatory):** WordCraft contains **no Microsoft, Adobe, Avid or Autodesk iconography, images, templates, themes, clip art, fonts or other assets**. Every asset is original work by WordCraft contributors or third-party material under an open licence (OSI open source, public domain / CC0, or a Creative Commons licence that allows redistribution). Screenshots of other vendors' software are never committed. New font files go to [storytold/craft-fonts](https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts), not this repo. The one exception is the ArtCraft brand files in `docs/brand/`: ArtCraft trademarks, not open source, used under `docs/brand/LICENSE-brand.txt`.

Generated-in-code art is original and has no file to list: the UI icon set (`crates/ui-egui/src/icons.rs`), the sample documents and templates (`crates/engine/src/sample.rs`), the table and style previews, and theme colours (`crates/engine/src/cmd/design.rs`).

Third-party material

This product bundles third-party material under its own licenses, listed below. The per-file
list (author, source, license) is in ATTRIBUTION.md.

- Source Sans 3 and Source Serif 4 (assets/fonts/Source*) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. See assets/fonts/OFL-SourceSans3.txt, OFL-SourceSerif4.txt.
- Inter (assets/fonts/Inter-*.ttf) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. See assets/fonts/OFL-Inter.txt.
- JetBrains Mono (assets/fonts/JetBrainsMono-*.ttf) — SIL Open Font License 1.1. See assets/fonts/OFL-JetBrainsMono.txt.
- Japanese fonts (craft-fonts): builds made with craft-fonts (CRAFT_FONTS_DIR; all official
  releases) embed the fonts listed in
  https://github.com/storytold/craft-fonts/blob/main/ATTRIBUTION.md (BIZ UDPGothic, BIZ UDMincho,
  Shippori Mincho), under the SIL Open Font License 1.1. Their licence texts ship with the
  packages as OFL-<family>.txt. Builds without CRAFT_FONTS_DIR do not include them.
- English word list (assets/hyphenation/en-us.dic) — derived from the Moby Hyphenator II list by
  Grady Ward, public domain (Project Gutenberg eBook #3204). The hyphenation patterns
  (assets/hyphenation/en-us.pat) were trained from it and are released as CC0-1.0.

Rust dependencies (egui, eframe, vello_cpu, skrifa, harfrust, krilla, quick-xml, zip, image, …)
are permissively licensed (MIT/Apache-2.0/BSD/ISC/Zlib) and fetched through crates.io or pinned Git sources.
The craft-pdf writer and Krilla 0.8.2 backend are pinned to storytold/craft-libs revision
b926b5c03e256c86ca10934f58d03f628810b5b7. The backend retains its MIT OR Apache-2.0 licensing
and upstream bundled material; see that revision's licenses/pdf-writer/ and ATTRIBUTION.md.
The backend patch prevents finalization during an unwind and rejects a writer after failed
cleanup. WordCraft enables only writing; PDF import and its image decoders are not enabled.

No Microsoft, Adobe, Avid or Autodesk icons, images, templates or artwork are included.

Bundled egui font icons

The canonical egui0.37 dependency includes egui-icons.ttf (a small subset of emoji-icon-font)
through default_fonts, and emoji-icon-font.ttf through monochrome_emoji_fonts. Both are embedded
by the UI font definitions. Their upstream project is https://github.com/jslegers/emoji-icon-font;
the exact copyright and permission notice from the pinned egui dependency follows.

The OFL allows the licensed fonts to be used, studied, modified and
redistributed freely as long as they are not sold by themselves. The
fonts, including any derivative works, can be bundled, embedded, 
redistributed and/or sold with any software provided that any reserved
names are not used by derivative works. The fonts and derivatives,
however, cannot be released under any other type of license. The

to any document created using the fonts or their derivatives.

Holder(s) under this license and clearly marked as such. This may
include source files, build scripts and documentation.

"Modified Version" refers to any derivative made by adding to, deleting,
or substituting -- in part or in whole -- any of the components of the

ArtCraft brand assets: license

The ArtCraft name, wordmark and mark (the "ArtCraft Marks"), including every file in this
directory in every color, size and format, are owned by the ArtCraft Team. They are trademarks
and are NOT open source. They are not covered by WordCraft's MIT OR Apache-2.0 license, and nothing in
that license, in NOTICE or in any other file of this repository grants any right to them.

Permission

The ArtCraft Team grants a limited, non-exclusive, non-transferable, revocable, royalty-free
permission to use the ArtCraft Marks only:

  1. as part of copies of this repository; and
  2. within WordCraft itself, its documentation and its official builds, to identify WordCraft as made
     by the ArtCraft team and community and to link to getartcraft.com and its community.

Conditions

The permission holds only while every one of these is true:

  - The marks are used exactly as supplied in this directory. Do not redraw, recolor, crop,
    stretch, rotate, animate, outline or add effects to them, extract parts of them, or combine
    them with any other name, mark or artwork.
  - They are used only in the context of WordCraft, and only to say that the ArtCraft team makes it.
    They must never suggest that the ArtCraft Team made, sponsors, endorses, certifies or supports
    any other product, service, fork, modified version, plug-in, organization or person.
  - Neither the marks nor the ArtCraft name appear in the name, logo, icon, domain, social media
    handle, app-store listing, advertising or marketing of anything other than WordCraft.

Forks and modified versions

If you distribute or publish a modified version of WordCraft, or any work derived from it, you must
remove the ArtCraft Marks from it (or replace them with your own) and must not call it ArtCraft or
present it as an ArtCraft product. You may say, in plain text, that your work is based on WordCraft by
the ArtCraft team. A fork kept only to propose changes back to this repository may keep the marks
unchanged.

Everything else

Any use not expressly permitted here needs prior written permission from the ArtCraft Team
(https://getartcraft.com/). The permission ends automatically for anyone who breaks these terms.
All other rights are reserved.
